If there is an existing association between the job plan and a safety plan when the job plan is used on the asset or location, Maximo also copies the safety plan to the work order. In addition, Maximo automatically copies a single measurement point if there is an existing association between the asset on the work order and the point name on the job plan.</p></div></div></span></span></td></tr><tr><td style="vertical-align: top; react-text: 3782 -->(<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3783 -->query<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3784 -->)<!-- /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>If set to 1, this will instruct the api to work with the "lean" json format as opposed to the namespaced json that it produces by default. This option is supported only starting 7601 release of the Maximo base. Its preferred to use this format as it reduces the size of the json payload.</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3791 -->x-public-uri<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!-- react-text: 3793 -->string<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3794 --> <!-- /react-text --></div><div /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>The x-public-uri</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3806 -->maxauth<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!-- react-text: 3808 -->string<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3809 --> <!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in"><!-- react-text: 3812 -->(<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3813 -->header<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3814 -->)<!-- /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>The maxauth header for authentication</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3821 -->x-method-override<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!-- react-text: 3823 -->string<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3824 --> <!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in"><!-- react-text: 3827 -->(<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3828 -->header<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3829 -->)<!-- /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>Due to constraints from browser the HTTP POST method is re-used for creates and updates and deletes.This request header is used to convey the specifics of the POST request. PATCH is used for update. DELETE is used for deleting the resource. BULK is used for supporting bulk operations.If this header is not present, the POST request is treated as a create request.</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3836 -->x-allow-events<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!-- react-text: 3838 -->integer<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3839 --> <!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in"><!-- react-text: 3842 -->(<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3843 -->header<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3844 -->)<!-- /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>For a create/update/delete, if the request wants to suppress the object events, this request header needs to be set to 0. By default all events are fired unless programmatically stopped.</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3851 -->transactionid<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!-- react-text: 3853 -->string<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3854 --> <!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__deprecated"></div><div class="parameter__in"><!-- react-text: 3857 -->(<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3858 -->header<!-- /react-text --><!-- react-text: 3859 -->)<!-- /react-text --></div></td><td class="col parameters-col_description"><div class="renderedMarkdown"><div><p>This is used to prevent de-duplication for create/update/delete. If this header is present then the value is compared to a list of committed transaction ids.If it has a match, then it is considered as a duplicate transaction and an HTTP conflict error is generated.</p></div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="col parameters-col_name"><div class="parameter__name"><!-- react-text: 3866 -->properties<!-- /react-text --></div><div class="parameter__type"><!--
